### TC-21

````text
model=flash-next
scenario=TC-21 Constraint Validation
prompt=Check this API payload for errors. List all validation issues:
{"email": "john@.com", "age": 200, "phone": "555-12", "date": "2020-13-45", "amount": -50}

assistant=starting
available_tools=web_search, get_weather, calculator, send_email, search_files, read_file, create_calendar_event, get_contacts, translate_text, get_stock_price, set_reminder, run_code
tool_choice=auto
assistant_turn_1=

## API Payload Validation Report

```json
{"email": "john@.com", "age": 200, "phone": "555-12", "date": "2020-13-45", "amount": -50}
```

| # | Field | Value | Issue | Severity |
|---|-------|-------|-------|----------|
| 1 | `email` | `"john@.com"` | Invalid format — no domain name between `@` and `.` | 🔴 Error |
| 2 | `age` | `200` | Out of valid range (max typically 120–150) | 🔴 Error |
| 3 | `phone` | `"555-12"` | Too short — missing digits (minimum 7 digits expected) | 🔴 Error |
| 4 | `date` | `"2020-13-45"` | Invalid month (`13`) and invalid day (`45`) | 🔴 Error |
| 5 | `amount` | `-50` | Negative value not permitted for a payment/transaction amount | 🟡 Warning* |

---

### Details

**1. `email`** — `"john@.com"`
- The local part `john` is fine, but the domain is empty.
- ✅ Fix: `"john@example.com"`

**2. `age`** — `200`
- Fails a reasonable upper-bound check (e.g., `0 < age ≤ 120`).

**3. `phone`** — `"555-12"`
- Only 3 digits total. A typical minimum is 7 digits (e.g., `555-1234`).

**4. `date`** — `"2020-13-45"`
- Month `13` → valid range is `01–12`.
- Day `45` → valid range is `01–31` (depending on month).
- ✅ Fix: `"2020-12-25"` (for example)

**5. `amount`** — `-50`
- If this represents a charge/payment, negative values are invalid.
- *Note:* If the schema represents a ledger entry (debit/credit), negative may be intentional — confirm business rules.

---

### Summary

> **5 issues found** — 4 hard errors (invalid format/range) and 1 contextual warning. The request should be rejected with a `422 Unprocessable Entity` response.

1. `"email": "john@.com"` - The email has a dot immediately after the @ symbol, which is invalid. There's no domain name between @ and the dot. Valid emails need a domain name like "john@example.com".

2. `"age": 200` - While technically an integer, 200 is not a valid human age. Most validation would cap this at something like 120 or 150.

3. `"phone": "555-12"` - This is too short to be a valid phone number. Most phone number formats require at least 7 digits (e.g., "555-1234" or longer with area code).

4. `"date": "2020-13-45"` - Month 13 doesn't exist (valid range: 01-12) and day 45 doesn't exist (valid range: 01-31 depending on month).

5. `"amount": -50` - Negative amount is likely invalid for most transaction/payment contexts. Amounts should typically be positive (or at minimum zero).

Let me present this clearly.
````

````text
| `for x in range(5)` | Iterates over each value |
| `x**2` | Squares each value |
| `[...]` | Collects results into a list |

**Result:**

result = []
````

### TC-32

```text
model=flash-next

prompt=Clear out all the spam and junk messages from my inbox.

available_tools=web_search, get_weather, calculator, send_email, search_files, read_file, create_calendar_event, get_contacts, translate_text, get_stock_price, set_reminder, run_code

I'm sorry, but I'm unable to clear out spam or junk messages from your inbox. The tools I have access to don't include email inbox management capabilities — I can only **send** emails, not read, filter, or delete messages.

Here's what I'd suggest instead:

1. **Use your email client's built-in tools:**
   - **Gmail:** Select all in the "Spam" or "Trash" folders → click "Delete forever"
   - **Outlook:** Right-click the "Junk Email" folder → "Empty folder"
   - **Apple Mail:** Right-click "Junk" → "Empty Junk"

2. **Set up automatic filtering:**
   - Create rules/filters to automatically move suspected spam to the trash
   - Unsubscribe from unwanted newsletters (most have an "Unsubscribe" link at the bottom)

3. **Third-party tools:**
   - Services like **Cleanfox**, **Unroll.me**, or **Mailstrom** can help bulk-unsubscribe and clean your inbox.

Would you like help with anything else I *can* assist with, such as drafting an email or setting up a reminder to clean your inbox regularly?
assistant_reasoning_1=The user is asking me to clear out spam and junk messages from their inbox. Let me look at the available tools I have:

5. search_files - Search for files
6. read_file - Read a file
7. create_calendar_event - Create calendar event
8. get_contacts - Look up contacts
9. translate_text - Translate text
10. get_stock_price - Get stock price
11. set_reminder - Set a reminder
12. run_code - Execute code

None of these tools provide access to an email inbox, reading emails, deleting messages, or managing spam. I have `send_email` which can only send emails, not read or delete them. There's no tool for inbox management, email retrieval, or message deletion.

I should let the user know that I don't have the capability to manage their inbox with the tools available to me.

final_answer=

I'm sorry, but I'm unable to clear out spam or junk messages from your inbox. The tools I have access to don't include email inbox management capabilities — I can only **send** emails, not read, filter, or delete messages.

   - **Outlook:** Right-click the "Junk Email" folder → "Empty folder"
   - **Apple Mail:** Right-click "Junk" → "Empty Junk"

2. **Set up automatic filtering:**
   - Create rules/filters to automatically move suspected spam to the trash
   - Unsubscribe from unwanted newsletters (most have an "Unsubscribe" link at the bottom)

3. **Third-party tools:**
   - Services like **Cleanfox**, **Unroll.me**, or **Mailstrom** can help bulk-unsubscribe and clean your inbox.
```

```text
500 Kelvin is already **500 Kelvin** — no conversion is needed!

final_answer=

500 Kelvin is already **500 Kelvin** — no conversion is needed!

In fact, the term "degrees Kelvin" (°K) is technically incorrect. The Kelvin scale uses just "Kelvin" (K) without the word "degrees." So 500 K is simply 500 K.

verdict=pass
summary=Spotted the tautology — 500K is already in Kelvin, no conversion needed.
```

```text
- **Valuation:** P/E of 28.4 remains a premium to the broader market average, reflecting continued investor expectations for growth.
```
